Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on automata structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 787. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 897 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to automata theory, we decompose the problem into 787 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 897. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
